@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_prepare"
defg mavgTN_true(a, b,top_num,ascending,tiesMethod){
	x = array(type(a[0])).append!(a)
	y = b
	// y  = nullFill(y,min(y)-1)
	if(ascending)
		cur = y.size()-y.count()
	else 
		cur= 0
	if(tiesMethod[0] == 'oldest'){
		return x[isort(y,ascending[0])[cur:(cur+min(top_num[0], y.count()))]].avg()	
	}else if(tiesMethod[0] == 'latest'){
		x2 = x[size(x):0]
		y2 = y[size(y):0]		
		return x2[isort(y2,ascending[0])[cur:(cur+min(top_num[0], y2.count()))]].avg()	
	}else if(tiesMethod[0] == 'all'){
		rank_y = rank(y,ascending = ascending[0])
		rank_y = rank_y[rank_y<top_num[0]]
		get_number = count(rank_y)
		if(get_number > top_num[0]){
			top_num_all = get_number
		}else{
			top_num_all = top_num
		}
		return x[isort(y,ascending[0])[cur:(cur+min(top_num_all[0], y.count()))]].avg()	
	}else{
		throw("error")
	}
}

def my_mavgTopN(a, b,window,top_num,ascending=true,tiesMethod='oldest'){
	return  moving(mavgTN_true, [a,b,take(top_num,b.size()),take(ascending,b.size()),take(tiesMethod,b.size())], window,1)	
}

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_S_array_normal"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double,`decimal32,`decimal64, `decimal128]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double,`decimal32,`decimal64, `decimal128]
a1 = rand(10,10)
b1 = rand(10,10)
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		if(b_type == `float || b_type == `double){
			b1 = rand((rand(1.0,3) + 1) join (rand(1.0,4) + 2) join rand(10.0,3),10)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)
		}else if(b_type == `decimal32 || b_type == `decimal64 || b_type == `decimal128){
			b1 = rand((rand(1.0,3) + 1) join (rand(1.0,4) + 2) join rand(10.0,3),10)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1,3)
		}else{
			b1 = rand(10,10)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)
		}
		if(a_type == `decimal32 || a_type == `decimal64 || a_type == `decimal128){
			a = funcByName(a_type)(a1,3)
		}else{
			a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		}
		
		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		assert 2,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		assert 4,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		assert 5,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		assert 6,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		assert 7,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		assert 8,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		assert 9,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		assert 10,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		assert 11,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		assert 12,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)

		re_16 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false,"all")

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		assert 71,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		assert 72,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)
	}
}

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_has_null_S_array_normal"

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_all_null_S_array_normal"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double,`decimal32,`decimal64, `decimal128]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double,`decimal32,`decimal64, `decimal128]
a1 = take(int(),10)
b1 = rand(10,10)
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		if(b_type == `float || b_type == `double){
			b1 = rand((rand(1.0,3) + 1) join (rand(1.0,4) + 2) join rand(10.0,3),10)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)
		}else if(b_type == `decimal32 || b_type == `decimal64 || b_type == `decimal128){
			b1 = rand((rand(1.0,3) + 1) join (rand(1.0,4) + 2) join rand(10.0,3),10)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1,3)
		}else{
			b1 = rand(10,10)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)
		}
		if(a_type == `decimal32 || a_type == `decimal64 || a_type == `decimal128){
			a = funcByName(a_type)(a1,3)
		}else{
			a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		}
		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		assert 2,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		assert 4,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		assert 5,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		assert 6,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		assert 7,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		assert 8,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		assert 9,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		assert 10,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		assert 11,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		assert 12,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

		ex_13 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		assert 13,each(eqObj,re_13,ex_13)

		ex_14 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false)
		re_14 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false)
		assert 14,each(eqObj,re_14,ex_14)

		ex_15 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false)
		re_15 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false)
		assert 15,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_16 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false)
		re_16 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false)
		assert 16,each(eqObj,re_16,ex_16)

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false)
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false)
		assert 17,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false)
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false)
		assert 18,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true,"latest")
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true,"latest")
		assert 19,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true,"latest")
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true,"latest")
		assert 20,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true,"latest")
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true,"latest")
		assert 21,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true,"latest")
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true,"latest")
		assert 22,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"latest")
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"latest")
		assert 23,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"latest")
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"latest")
		assert 24,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"latest")
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"latest")
		assert 25,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_S_array_normal_has_null"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double,`decimal32,`decimal64, `decimal128]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double,`decimal32,`decimal64, `decimal128]
a1 = rand(10,10)
b1 = 1 2 3 join NULL join 5 6 join take(int(),2) join 9 10
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		if(b_type == `float || b_type == `double){
			b1 = rand((rand(1.0,3) + 1) join (rand(1.0,4) + 2) join rand(10.0,1) join NULL join NULL,10)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)
		}else if(b_type == `decimal32 || b_type == `decimal64 || b_type == `decimal128){
			b1 = rand((rand(1.0,3) + 1) join (rand(1.0,4) + 2) join rand(10.0,1) join NULL join NULL,10)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1,3)
		}else{
			b1 = 1 2 3 join NULL join 5 6 join take(int(),2) join 9 10
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)
		}
		if(a_type == `decimal32 || a_type == `decimal64 || a_type == `decimal128){
			a = funcByName(a_type)(a1,3)
		}else{
			a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		}
		
		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		assert 2,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		assert 4,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		assert 5,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		assert 6,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		assert 8,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		assert 9,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		assert 10,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		assert 11,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true,"all")
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true,"all")
		assert 58,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"all")
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"all")
		assert 59,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"all")
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"all")
		assert 60,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"all")
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"all")
		assert 61,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true,"all")
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true,"all")
		assert 62,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true,"all")
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true,"all")
		assert 63,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false,"all")
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false,"all")
		assert 64,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false,"all")
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false,"all")
		assert 65,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false,"all")
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false,"all")
		assert 66,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

		ex_13 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false,"all")
		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false,"all")
		assert 67,each(eqObj,re_13,ex_13)

		ex_14 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false,"all")
		re_14 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false,"all")
		assert 68,each(eqObj,re_14,ex_14)

		ex_15 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false,"all")
		re_15 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false,"all")
		assert 69,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_16 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false,"all")
		re_16 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false,"all")
		assert 70,each(eqObj,re_16,ex_16)

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		assert 71,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		assert 72,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)
	}
}

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_S_array_normal_has_duplicate"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double,`decimal32,`decimal64, `decimal128]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double,`decimal32,`decimal64, `decimal128]
a1 = rand(10,10)
b1 = take(rand(10,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10,1)[0],2) join take(rand(10,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10,1)[0],2)
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		if(b_type == `float || b_type == `double){
			b1 = take(rand(10.0,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10.0,1)[0],2) join take(rand(10.0,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10.0,1)[0],2)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)
		}else if(b_type == `decimal32 || b_type == `decimal64 || b_type == `decimal128){
			b1 = take(rand(10.0,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10.0,1)[0],2) join take(rand(10.0,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10.0,1)[0],2)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1,3)
		}else{
			b1 = take(rand(10,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10,1)[0],2) join take(rand(10,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10,1)[0],2)
			b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)
		}
		if(a_type == `decimal32 || a_type == `decimal64 || a_type == `decimal128){
			a = funcByName(a_type)(a1,3)
		}else{
			a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		}

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		assert 2,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		assert 4,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		assert 5,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		assert 6,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		assert 7,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		assert 8,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		assert 9,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		assert 10,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		assert 11,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		assert 12,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

		ex_13 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		assert 13,each(eqObj,re_13,ex_13)

		ex_14 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false)
		re_14 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false)
		assert 14,each(eqObj,re_14,ex_14)

		ex_15 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false)
		re_15 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false)
		assert 15,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_15 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false,"all")

		assert 69,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_16 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false,"all")

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")

		assert 71,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		assert 72,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)
		
	}
}

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_S_array_<1_has_duplicate"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`float,`double]
type2 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`float,`double]
a1 = rand(10,10)
b1 = (take(rand(10,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10,1)[0],2) join take(rand(10,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10,1)[0],2))\10
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		assert 2,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		assert 4,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		assert 5,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		assert 6,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		assert 8,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		assert 9,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		assert 10,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		assert 11,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)

		ex_13 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		assert 13,each(eqObj,re_13,ex_13)

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"oldest")
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"oldest")
		assert 53,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"oldest")
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"oldest")
		assert 54,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true,"all")
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true,"all")
		assert 55,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true,"all")
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true,"all")
		assert 56,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true,"all")
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true,"all")
		assert 57,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true,"all")
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true,"all")
		assert 58,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"all")
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"all")
		assert 59,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"all")
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"all")
		assert 60,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"all")
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"all")
		assert 61,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true,"all")
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true,"all")
		assert 62,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true,"all")
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true,"all")
		assert 63,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false,"all")
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false,"all")
		assert 64,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false,"all")
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false,"all")
		assert 65,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false,"all")
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false,"all")
		assert 66,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

		ex_13 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false,"all")
		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false,"all")
		assert 67,each(eqObj,re_13,ex_13)

		ex_14 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false,"all")
		re_14 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false,"all")
		assert 68,each(eqObj,re_14,ex_14)

		ex_15 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false,"all")
		re_15 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false,"all")
		assert 69,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_16 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false,"all")
		re_16 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false,"all")
		assert 70,each(eqObj,re_16,ex_16)

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		assert 71,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		assert 72,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)
	}
}

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_S_array_<0_has_duplicate"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`float,`double]
type2 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`float,`double]
a1 = rand(10,10)
b1 = -(take(rand(10,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10,1)[0],2) join take(rand(10,1)[0],3) join take(rand(10,1)[0],2))
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		assert 2,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		assert 4,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		assert 5,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		assert 6,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		assert 7,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		assert 8,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		assert 9,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		assert 10,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		assert 11,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		assert 12,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

		ex_13 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		assert 13,each(eqObj,re_13,ex_13)

		ex_14 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false)
		re_14 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false)
		assert 14,each(eqObj,re_14,ex_14)

		ex_15 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false)
		re_15 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false)
		assert 15,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_16 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false)
		re_16 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false)
		assert 16,each(eqObj,re_16,ex_16)

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false)
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false)
		assert 17,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_S_array_<0"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`float,`double]
type2 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`float,`double]
a1 = rand(10,10)
b1 =-rand(10,10)
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)

		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)

		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true,"all")
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true,"all")
		assert 56,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true,"all")
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true,"all")
		assert 57,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true,"all")
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true,"all")
		assert 58,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"all")
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"all")
		assert 59,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"all")
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"all")
		assert 60,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"all")
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"all")
		assert 61,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true,"all")
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true,"all")
		assert 62,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true,"all")
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true,"all")
		assert 63,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false,"all")
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false,"all")
		assert 64,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false,"all")
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false,"all")
		assert 65,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false,"all")
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false,"all")
		assert 66,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

		ex_13 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false,"all")
		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false,"all")
		assert 67,each(eqObj,re_13,ex_13)

		ex_14 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false,"all")
		re_14 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false,"all")
		assert 68,each(eqObj,re_14,ex_14)

		ex_15 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false,"all")
		re_15 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false,"all")
		assert 69,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_16 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false,"all")
		re_16 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false,"all")
		assert 70,each(eqObj,re_16,ex_16)

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		assert 71,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		assert 72,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)
		
	}
}

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_over_1024_has_null_S_array_normal"
type1 = [`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
type2 = [`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
a1 = take(1,1065) join take(int(),3) join take(1,2) 
b1 = 1..1070
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)
		
		ex_1 =  take(1,1070)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,30,20,true)
		assert 1,each(eq,re_1,ex_1)

		assert 3,each(eq,re_3,ex_3)

		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,1071,300,true)
		assert 4,each(eq,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_2 = take(1,1065) join take(int(),3) join take(1,2)

		assert 28,each(eq,re_1,ex_1)

		assert 29,each(eq,re_2,ex_2)

		assert 31,each(eq,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = take(1,1065) join take(int(),3) join take(1,2) 
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,1071,1,false,"oldest")
		assert 32,each(eq,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 =  take(1,1070)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,1071,1071,false,"oldest")
		assert 33,each(eq,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 =  take(1,1070)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,1070,300,false,"oldest")
		assert 34,each(eq,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 =  take(1,1065) join take(int(),3) join take(1,2)

re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,30,30,false)
assert 12,each(eq,re_3,ex_3)

ex_4 = take(1,1048580)
re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,1048581,300,false)
assert 13,each(eq,re_4,ex_4)

ex_5 = take(1,1048575) join take(int(),3) join take(1,2)
re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,1048581,1,false)
assert 14,each(eq,re_5,ex_5)

ex_6 = take(1,1048580)
re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,1048581,1048581,false)
assert 15,each(eq,re_6,ex_6)

ex_7 = take(1,1048580)
re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,1048580,300,false)
assert 16,each(eq,re_7,ex_7)

ex_8 = take(1,1048575) join take(int(),3) join take(1,2)
re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,1048580,1,false)
assert 17,each(eq,re_8,ex_8)

ex_9 = take(1,1048580)
re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,1048580,1048580,false)
assert 18,each(eq,re_9,ex_9)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_bigArray_all_null_S_array_normal"
a = bigarray(int,0,10000000).append!(take(int(),1048580))
b = bigarray(int,0,10000000).append!(rand(1000,1048580))

re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,30,20,true)

//--------------------------------------support matrix----------------------------------------------------
def my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,window,top_num,ascending=true,tiesMethod='oldest'){	
	res = []
	for (i in 0..(a.cols()-1)){	
		a_0 = a[i]
		res.append!(moving(mavgTN_true, [a_0,b,take(top_num,b.size()),take(ascending,b.size()),take(tiesMethod,b.size())], window,1))
	}
	return matrix(res) 
}

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_matrix_S_array_normal"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
a1 = rand(10,30)$10:3
b1 = rand(10,10)

for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,2,true)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_matrix_S_array_normal_view"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
a1 = rand(10,30)$10:3
b1 = rand(10,10)

for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1).loc(colFilter=[true,true,false],view=true)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,1,true)
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		assert 2,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,3,true)
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,3,true)
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		assert 4,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,1,true)
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		assert 5,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,11,true)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		assert 6,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,3,true)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		assert 7,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,1,true)
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		assert 8,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,10,true)
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		assert 9,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,2,false)
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		assert 10,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,1,false)
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		assert 11,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,3,false)
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		assert 12,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

		ex_13 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,3,false)
		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		assert 13,each(eqObj,re_13,ex_13)

		ex_14 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,1,false)
		re_14 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false)
		assert 14,each(eqObj,re_14,ex_14)

		ex_15 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,11,false)
		re_15 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false)
		assert 15,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_16 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,3,false)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_matrix_has_null_S_array_normal"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
a1 = rand(1..10 join take(int(),5),30)$10:3
b1 = rand(10,10)
		
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,2,true)

		re_15 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false,"oldest")
		assert 51,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_16 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,3,false,"oldest")
		re_16 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false,"oldest")
		assert 52,each(eqObj,re_16,ex_16)

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,1,false,"oldest")
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"oldest")
		assert 53,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,10,false,"oldest")
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"oldest")
		assert 54,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,2,true,"all")
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true,"all")
		assert 55,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,1,true,"all")
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true,"all")
		assert 56,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,3,true,"all")

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,3,true,"all")

		assert 58,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,1,true,"all")
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"all")
		assert 59,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,11,true,"all")
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"all")
		assert 60,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,3,true,"all")
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"all")
		assert 61,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,1,true,"all")
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true,"all")
		assert 62,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,10,true,"all")
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true,"all")
		assert 63,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,2,false,"all")
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false,"all")
		assert 64,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)

		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false,"all")

		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false,"all")
		assert 66,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_matrix_all_null_S_array_normal"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
a1 = take(int(),30)$10:3
b1 = rand(10,10)
	
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,1,true)
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		assert 2,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,3,true)
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,3,true)
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		assert 4,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,1,true)
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		assert 5,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,11,11,true)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		assert 6,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,3,true)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		assert 7,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,1,true)
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		assert 8,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,10,10,true)
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		assert 9,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN_matrix(a, b,3,2,false)
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		assert 10,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)

type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
a1 = take(int(),30)$10:3
b1 = rand(10,10)
	
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1).loc(colFilter=[true,true,false],view=true)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)

}

//S is matrix
def my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,window,top_num,ascending=true,tiesMethod='oldest'){	
	res = []
	for (i in 0..(a.cols()-1)){	
		a_0 = a[i]
		b_0 = b[i]
		res.append!(moving(mavgTN_true, [a_0,b_0,take(top_num,b_0.size()),take(ascending,b_0.size()),take(tiesMethod,b_0.size())], window,1))
	}
	return matrix(res) 
}

b1 = rand(10,30)$10:3

		assert 70,each(eqObj,re_16,ex_16)

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false,"all")
		assert 71,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false,"all")
		assert 72,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)
	}

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_matrix_all_null_S_matrix_normal"
type1 = [`bool,`short,`char,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
type2 = [`bool,`char,`short,`int,`long,`date,`month,`time,`minute,`second,`datetime,`timestamp,`nanotime,`nanotimestamp,`float,`double]
a1 = take(int(),30)$10:3
b1 = rand(10,30)$10:3
	
for(a_type in type1){
	for(b_type in type2){	
		a = funcByName(a_type)(a1)
		b = funcByName(b_type)(b1)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,2,true)
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true)
		assert 1,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,1,true)
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true)
		assert 2,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,3,true)
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true)
		assert 3,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,3,true)
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true)
		assert 4,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,1,true)
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true)
		assert 5,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,11,true)
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true)
		assert 6,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,3,true)
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true)
		assert 7,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,1,true)
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true)
		assert 8,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,10,true)
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true)
		assert 9,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,2,false)
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false)
		assert 10,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,1,false)
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false)
		assert 11,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,3,false)
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false)
		assert 12,each(eqObj,re_12,ex_12)

		ex_13 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,3,false)
		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false)
		assert 13,each(eqObj,re_13,ex_13)

		ex_14 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,1,false)
		re_14 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,false)
		assert 14,each(eqObj,re_14,ex_14)

		ex_15 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,11,false)
		re_15 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,false)
		assert 15,each(eqObj,re_15,ex_15)

		ex_16 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,3,false)
		re_16 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,false)
		assert 16,each(eqObj,re_16,ex_16)

		ex_17 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,1,false)
		re_17 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,false)
		assert 17,each(eqObj,re_17,ex_17)

		ex_18 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,10,false)
		re_18 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,false)
		assert 18,each(eqObj,re_18,ex_18)

		ex_1 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,2,true,"latest")
		re_1 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,true,"latest")
		assert 19,each(eqObj,re_1,ex_1)
		
		ex_2 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,1,true,"latest")
		re_2 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,true,"latest")
		assert 20,each(eqObj,re_2,ex_2)
		
		ex_3 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,3,true,"latest")
		re_3 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,true,"latest")
		assert 21,each(eqObj,re_3,ex_3)

		ex_4 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,3,true,"latest")
		re_4 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,true,"latest")
		assert 22,each(eqObj,re_4,ex_4)

		ex_5 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,1,true,"latest")
		re_5 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,1,true,"latest")
		assert 23,each(eqObj,re_5,ex_5)

		ex_6 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,11,true,"latest")
		re_6 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,11,true,"latest")
		assert 24,each(eqObj,re_6,ex_6)

		ex_7 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,3,true,"latest")
		re_7 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,3,true,"latest")
		assert 25,each(eqObj,re_7,ex_7)

		ex_8 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,1,true,"latest")
		re_8 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,1,true,"latest")
		assert 26,each(eqObj,re_8,ex_8)

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,10,true,"latest")
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true,"latest")
		assert 27,each(eqObj,re_9,ex_9)

		ex_10 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,2,false,"latest")
		re_10 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,2,false,"latest")
		assert 28,each(eqObj,re_10,ex_10)
		
		ex_11 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,1,false,"latest")
		re_11 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,1,false,"latest")
		assert 29,each(eqObj,re_11,ex_11)
		
		ex_12 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,3,3,false,"latest")
		re_12 = mavgTopN(a, b,3,3,false,"latest")

		ex_9 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,10,10,true,"all")
		re_9 = mavgTopN(a, b,10,10,true,"all")

		re_13 = mavgTopN(a, b,11,3,false,"all")

		ex_14 = my_mavgTopN_matrix_S_matrix_S_matrix(a, b,11,1,false,"all")

	}

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_array_S_Matrix",exception = 1 
a = 1..5
b = 1..10$5:2
re = mavgTopN(a,b,3,2)
ex = mavgTopN(a,b[0],3,2)
//assert 1,eqObj(re,ex)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_setIndexedMatrix"
m=matrix(1..5, 11..15)
n = m
m.rename!(2020.01.01..2020.01.05, `A`B)
m.setIndexedMatrix!();
re = mavgTopN(m,1..5,2,1,true)
ex = my_mavgTopN_matrix(n,1..5,2,1,true)
assert 1,each(eqObj,re,ex)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_matrix_rename"
m=matrix(1..5, 11..15)
n = m
m.rename!(2020.01.01..2020.01.05, `A`B)
re = mavgTopN(m,1..5,2,1,true)
ex = my_mavgTopN_matrix(n,1..5,2,1,true)
assert 1,each(eqObj,re,ex)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_X_indexedSeries"

re = mavgTopN(a,1..10,2,1,true)
ex = my_mavgTopN(a[0],1..10,2,1,true)
assert 1,each(eqObj,re[0],ex)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_in_memory_table"
n = 3000
ids = 1..3000 
dates = take(2021.01.01..2021.10.01,n)
prices = rand(1000,n)
vals = rand(1000,n)
t = table(ids as id,dates as date,prices as price,vals as val)
re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true) from t 
ex = mavgTopN(prices,ids,10,5,true)
assert 1,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true,"oldest") from t 
ex = mavgTopN(prices,ids,10,5,true,"oldest")
assert 2,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true,"latest") from t 
ex = mavgTopN(prices,ids,10,5,true,"latest")
assert 3,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true,"all") from t 
ex = mavgTopN(prices,ids,10,5,true,"all")
assert 4,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_in_memory_table_where"
n = 3000
ids = 1..3000 
dates = take(2021.01.01..2021.10.01,n)
prices = rand(1000,n)
vals = rand(1000,n)
t = table(ids as id,dates as date,prices as price,vals as val)
re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true) from t where date>2021.05.01 
tmp = select * from t where date>2021.05.01
prices = exec price from tmp
ids = exec id from tmp
ex = mavgTopN(prices,ids,10,5,true)
assert 1,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_in_memory_table_context_by"
n = 3000
ids = 1..3000 
dates = take(2021.01.01..2021.10.01,n)
prices = rand(1000,n)
vals = rand(1000,n)
syms = take(`a`b,n)
t = table(ids as id,dates as date,prices as price,vals as val,syms as sym)
re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true) from t context by sym
tmp_a = select * from t where sym = `a
prices_a = exec price from tmp_a
ids_a = exec id from tmp_a
ex_a = mavgTopN(prices_a,ids_a,10,5,true)

tmp_b = select * from t where sym = `b
prices_b = exec price from tmp_b
ids_b = exec id from tmp_b
ex_b= mavgTopN(prices_b,ids_b,10,5,true)

ex = ex_a.append!(ex_b)
assert 1,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)


@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_in_memory_table_context_by_where"
n = 3000
ids = 1..3000 
dates = take(2021.01.01..2021.10.01,n)
prices = rand(1000,n)
vals = rand(1000,n)
syms = take(`a`b,n)
t = table(ids as id,dates as date,prices as price,vals as val,syms as sym)
re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true) from t where date>2021.05.01 context by sym
tmp_a = select * from t where sym = `a and date>2021.05.01
prices_a = exec price from tmp_a
ids_a = exec id from tmp_a
ex_a = mavgTopN(prices_a,ids_a,10,5,true)

tmp_b = select * from t where sym = `b and date>2021.05.01
prices_b = exec price from tmp_b
ids_b = exec id from tmp_b
ex_b= mavgTopN(prices_b,ids_b,10,5,true)

ex = ex_a.append!(ex_b)
assert 1,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

@testing:case = "test_mavgTopN_in_partitioned_table"
n = 3000
ids = 1..3000 
dates = take(2021.01.01..2021.10.01,n)
prices = rand(1000,n)
vals = rand(1000,n)
t = table(ids as id,dates as date,prices as price,vals as val)
dbName = "dfs://test_mavgTopN_1"
if(existsDatabase(dbName))dropDB(dbName)
db = database(dbName,VALUE,1..5000)
pt = db.createPartitionedTable(t,"pt",`id).append!(t)
re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true) from pt 
ex = mavgTopN(prices,ids,10,5,true)
assert 1,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true,"oldest") from pt 
ex = mavgTopN(prices,ids,10,5,true,"oldest")
assert 2,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true,"latest") from pt 
ex = mavgTopN(prices,ids,10,5,true,"latest")
assert 3,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

re = select mavgTopN(price,id,10,5,true,"all") from pt 
ex = mavgTopN(prices,ids,10,5,true,"all")
assert 4,each(eqObj,re.values()[0],ex)

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_int",exception = 1 
mavgTopN(1..6, 3 0 2 2 1 3, 4, 3,true,tiesMethod = 1)

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_double",exception = 1 
mavgTopN(1..6, 3 0 2 2 1 3, 4, 3,true,tiesMethod = 1.1)

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_date",exception = 1 
mavgTopN(1..6, 3 0 2 2 1 3, 4, 3,true,tiesMethod = 2021.01.01)

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_error_string",exception = 1 
mavgTopN(1..6, 3 0 2 2 1 3, 4, 3,true,tiesMethod = "error")

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_vector",exception = 1 
mavgTopN(1..6, 3 0 2 2 1 3, 4, 3,true,tiesMethod = ["latest","oldest"])

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_matrix",exception = 1 
mavgTopN(1..6, 3 0 2 2 1 3, 4, 3,true,tiesMethod = 1..10$2:5)

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_dict",exception = 1 
mavgTopN(1..6, 3 0 2 2 1 3, 4, 3,true,tiesMethod = {1:"latest"})

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_table",exception = 1 
mavgTopN(1..6, 3 0 2 2 1 3, 4, 3,true,tiesMethod = table(["latest"] as a))

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_oldest"
x = 1..6
s = 3 0 2 2 1 3
re = mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,tiesMethod = 'oldest')
ex = my_mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,tiesMethod = 'oldest')
assert 1,each(eqObj,re,ex)

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_latest"
x = 1..6
s = 3 0 2 2 1 3
re = mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,tiesMethod = 'latest')
ex = my_mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,tiesMethod = 'latest')
assert 1,each(eqObj,re,ex)

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_all"
x = 1..6
s = 3 0 2 2 1 3
re = mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,tiesMethod = 'all')
ex = my_mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,tiesMethod = 'all')
assert 1,each(eqObj,re,ex)

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_ascending=true"
x = 1..6
s = 3 0 2 2 1 3
re = mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=true,tiesMethod = 'all')
ex = my_mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=true,tiesMethod = 'all')
assert 1,each(eqObj,re,ex)

re = mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=true,tiesMethod = 'latest')
ex = my_mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=true,tiesMethod = 'latest')
assert 2,each(eqObj,re,ex)

re = mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,tiesMethod = 'oldest')
ex = my_mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,tiesMethod = 'oldest')
assert 3,each(eqObj,re,ex)

@testing:case = "test_function_mavgTopN_tiesMethod_ascending=false"
x = 1..6
s = 3 0 2 2 1 3
re = mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=false,tiesMethod = 'all')
ex = my_mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=false,tiesMethod = 'all')
assert 1,each(eqObj,re,ex)

re = mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=false,tiesMethod = 'latest')
ex = my_mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=false,tiesMethod = 'latest')
assert 2,each(eqObj,re,ex)

re = mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=false,tiesMethod = 'oldest')
ex = my_mavgTopN(x, s, 4, 3,ascending=false,tiesMethod = 'oldest')
assert 3,each(eqObj,re,ex)
